# Skills · S · part 18

901 entries, sdd-build to sdlc-preflight. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sdd-build**: SDD 第④步 — 依 tasks.md 逐項開發，委派 mcp-developer agent 一次做一項，做完跑測試、打勾、停下來讓人確認。當使用者說「開始開發」「執行下一項任務」「依 tasks 實作」或執行 /sdd-build 時使用。 ([NL-Playground/mcp-lab](https://github.com/NL-Playground/mcp-lab/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sdd-build) · ★1)
- **sdd-build**: Use quando o plan.md da feature está ready e é hora de construir — \"executa o plano\", \"implementa a feature\", \"fase build\" — no SparkForge (perfil dev) ou num job do operador (perfil operator, sempre por change sandbox). ([EdgarSocrates98/spark-forge-aws](https://github.com/EdgarSocrates98/spark-forge-aws/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sdd-build) · 3 copies)
- **sdd-cascade**: Carry a large refactoring, or a new system whose algorithm must be invented, through a short chain of standalone skills — survey the code with the project's tools, optionally source the rules, agree the algorithm, agree the structure, translate it into code in a fresh agent, review by running the project's checks. Three gates are the owner's word; decisions live in one register. Use when the lifecycle marks a task :path :cascade, when the user invokes sdd-cascade or /sdd-cascade, or when a cascaded task must be resumed. ([zakyrion/sdd-flow](https://github.com/zakyrion/sdd-flow/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/sdd-cascade) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **sdd-change**: Update the spec chain when a requirement changed (amend) or code changed outside the workflow (reconcile). ([mariano-aguero/spec-driven-development-skill](https://github.com/mariano-aguero/spec-driven-development-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/sdd-change) · ★8)
- **sdd-change**: Use when implementing a meaningful repository change that requires an approved spec before code edits. ([jsuvic/agent-profile](https://github.com/jsuvic/agent-profile/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sdd-change) · ★3 · 37 copies)
- **sdd-change**: 통합 완료 후 변경 요청을 체계적으로 처리합니다. 영향 분석, 체크리스트 부분 갱신, TDD 델타 빌드, 회귀 검증을 포함하는 7 Phase 변경 관리 워크플로우입니다. Use when: "변경 요청", "기능 변경해줘", "수정 사항 반영", "change request", "modify feature" ([joypop-lguplus/claude-sdd](https://github.com/joypop-lguplus/claude-sdd/tree/HEAD/skills/sdd-change) · ★1)
- **sdd-check**: Internal — Verify that generated code is consistent with its approved SDD specification. Automatically called by sdd-gen. Can also be invoked manually to detect drift after manual code changes. ([viamus/claude-sdd-plugin](https://github.com/viamus/claude-sdd-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/sdd-check) · ★4)
- **sdd-clarify**: Refine a feature specification by identifying gaps, ambiguities, and edge cases. Use when the user says 'clarify spec', 'find ambiguity', 'what's missing', 'review spec for gaps', or wants to tighten a spec before planning. Runs after /sdd:specify. Gate obligatorio antes de planificar. ([jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude](https://github.com/jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sdd-clarify) · 6 copies)

- **sdd-clojure-flow**: Normalize prose or Clojure engineering requests into canonical Clojure instruction IR and enforce the persistent Research → Plan → Execute lifecycle. Use for engineering tasks, implementation requests, architecture or documentation changes, FLOW creation, resuming interrupted work, acceptance checks, or whenever the user invokes sdd-clojure-flow. - **sdd-close**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "close out REQ-…", "close out PR N", "write the PR body", "mark the PR ready", "the PR is merged, clean up", or "remove the merged branch and its worktree". Closes out a delivered change in its pull request, fixes the small outside defects and carries the leftover defects to the backlog, and after the merge removes the branch, its worktree and its findings file. Never merges or closes a pull request. Not for delivering (sdd-deliver) or working findings (sdd-triage). ([Cadasto/sdd-plugin](https://github.com/Cadasto/sdd-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/sdd-close))
- **sdd-closeout-phase**: Runs the close-out phase of one SDD spec: implements every proposal of the APPROVED retrospective plan with implementer and verifier agents, grouped by target repository and landed by each repository's own rules (direct commits in the spec store, a branch and one PR per code repository, in-place edits under ~/.claude), writes one outcome line per proposal in retrospective-plan.md, marks the plan CLOSED, and reports in the orchestrator contract. Used by the sdd-closeout-orchestrator agent, not directly from a main session. ([madmatt112/spec-workflow-mcp](https://github.com/madmatt112/spec-workflow-mcp/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/sdd-closeout-phase) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **sdd-code-index**: Indexes code via GitNexus (call graphs, symbols, flows) and maps it to requirements: symbol-level traceability, commit-to-requirement inference from git history, reconciliation prep.
